-f8 ports 999 x Sc (a:Glton MebG,al909 (hMigdO)Pdx Operator's Manual – Turn off the oxygen source when the ventilator is not in operation. O To prevent possible patient injury when using the ventilator with an oxygen concentrator, do not use a humidifier. Any humidifier system supplied with the concentrator must be removed before using the venti- lator. O The  Operat  control on the ventilator is not active when low-pressure oxygen is used. It is the operator's responsibility to control the oxygen setting. O To prevent possible patient injury, use low-pressure oxygen only in cases where the low-pressure source can provide an adequate level of oxygenation. O To prevent possible patient injury, ensure that an emergency backup oxygen supply (for example, a cylinder) is available in case the low- pressure oxygen source fails. O To calibrate the O2 sensor, discon- nect all O2 supplies. Calibration is performed at a concentration of 21%. O To protect the oxygen control system, do not supply both high- and low- pressure oxygen to the ventilator simultaneously.
